Conclusion: 该工作确立了片上纳米结构作为多功能量子光学平台的潜力，可实现光子路由、场局域化及原子-光子耦合增强，为量子应用中单原子操控与混合纳米光子-原子系统开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate a general mechanism for nanoscale lensing based on the phase gradient imposed by a single nanostructure scattering light in its near-field. We verify this effect using an optical waveguide on a substrate, with single atoms serving as quantum probes that sample the near-field intensity through their fluorescence. This quantum probing technique provides a unique, non-destructive approach to characterizing focused optical fields and reveals a 4-fold enhancement in single atom detection efficiency. This work establishes on-chip nanostructures as a multi-functional quantum optics platform that can efficiently route photons, localize fields, and enhance atom-photon coupling, offering new opportunities for trapping and manipulating single atoms and realizing hybrid nanophotonic-atomic systems for quantum applications.

</details>


### [98] [Radial Convex Geometry of Quantum States and Its Relation to Best Separable Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20050)
*Haonan Qiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子态的凸几何出发研究二体纠缠，定义了基于可分离态边界与量子态边界相对位置的几何纠缠空间量G(ρ)和相对纠缠度Q(ρ)，并将其应用于最佳可分离近似（BSA），推导出了一般界(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)] ≤ p_0 ≤ Q(ρ)以及纠缠分量纠缠空间大小不小于原始混合态的结论；对于双量子比特态，利用PPT判据给出了显式界(1-p)/(2p) ≤ p_0 ≤ Q(ρ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠的量化与刻画是量子信息理论的核心问题之一，但现有的纠缠度量往往依赖于具体判据或最小化程序，缺乏统一的几何直观。本文希望通过量子态空间的凸几何结构，为纠缠提供一种简洁的几何描述，并建立最佳可分离近似（BSA）、鲁棒性（robustness）与纠缠区域之间的内在联系。

Method: 以最大混合态为参考点，利用可分离态边界与量子态边界之间的相对位置，定义几何纠缠空间量G(ρ)=[1-L(ρ)]/L(ρ)，并进一步引入相对纠缠度Q(ρ)=[(1-p)/p]/G(ρ)。将该几何构造应用于最佳可分离近似（BSA）的最优分解，推导纠缠分量与原始混合态之间的几何参数不等式关系；对双量子比特态，利用PPT（偏转置）判据对几何参数进行显式求值。

Result: 推导出一般不等式(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)] ≤ p_0 ≤ Q(ρ)，并证明BSA的纠缠分量占据的纠缠空间大小不小于原始混合态，即[1-L_R]/L_R ≤ [1-L_B]/L_B。对双量子比特情形，BSA纠缠分量为纯纠缠态，通过PPT判据给出显式界(1-p)/(2p) ≤ p_0 ≤ Q(ρ)。

Conclusion: 上述结果提供了一种简洁的几何框架来描述BSA、鲁棒性与量子态空间纠缠区域之间的关系，为从凸几何角度理解纠缠度量之间的内在联系开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: We study bipartite entanglement from the convex geometry of quantum states. Taking the maximally mixed state as a reference point, we define a geometric entangled-space quantity $G(ρ)=[1-L(ρ)]/L(ρ)$ from the relative positions of the separable and quantum-state boundaries, and introduce a relative entanglement degree $Q(ρ)=[(1-p)/p]/G(ρ)$ by comparing it with the robustness relative to the maximally mixed state.
  We apply this geometric construction to the Best Separable Approximation (BSA). For the optimal decomposition, we derive the general bound $(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$ and show that the entangled component of the BSA has an entangled-space size no smaller than that of the original mixed state, namely $[1-L_R]/L_R\leq[1-L_B]/L_B$.
  For two-qubit states, the BSA entangled component is a pure entangled state. Using the PPT criterion, its geometric parameter can be evaluated explicitly, giving the bound $(1-p)/(2p)\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$. These results provide a simple geometric description of the relation between BSA, robustness, and the entangled region of the quantum-state space.

</details>


### [99] [PPT Entanglement with Correlated Catalysis: Monotones and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20063)
*Jingsong Ao,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在PPT资源理论中，当允许关联催化剂自由使用时，量子态操控仍受何种限制。研究发现即使任意关联催化剂也无法恢复混合态纠缠操控的渐近可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子催化剂可以克服原本不可能的量子态变换而不被消耗，允许其与输出相关会使其辅助能力大幅增强。这引发了一个基本问题：当此类关联催化剂自由可用时，纠缠理论中哪些态操控限制仍然存在？回答这一问题有助于理解混合态纠缠操控的根本局限。

Method: 作者在PPT（正部分转置）资源理论框架下展开研究。首先确定了正则化相对熵度量成为强超可加性的一般条件，并利用这些条件构建了无需催化剂任何知识即可约束关联催化PPT变换的单调量。关键证明包括正则化PPT相对熵的完全可加性和强超可加性。

Result: 证得正则化PPT相对熵是完全可加且强超可加的，解决了纠缠理论中的一个开放问题。更重要的是，这些约束表明即使任意关联催化剂也无法恢复渐近可逆性：对于显式构建的态，最优纠缠蒸馏率仍严格小于纠缠代价。

Conclusion: 即使是实质性的催化辅助也无法消除混合态纠缠操控的一些基本限制，这加深了我们对纠缠资源理论中催化辅助本质能力的理解。

Abstract: Quantum catalysts can overcome otherwise impossible quantum state transformations without being consumed, and allowing them to become correlated with the output makes this assistance substantially more powerful. This raises a fundamental question for entanglement theory: which limitations on state manipulation remain when such correlated catalysts are freely available? We answer this question in the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) resource theory, which allows a substantially broader class of operations than local operations and classical communication (LOCC). We identify general conditions under which regularized relative-entropy measures become strongly superadditive, and use them to construct monotones that constrain correlated catalytic PPT transformations without any knowledge of the catalyst. In particular, we prove that the regularized PPT relative entropy is fully additive and strongly superadditive, resolving an open problem in entanglement theory. Most importantly, these constraints show that even arbitrary correlated catalysts cannot restore asymptotic reversibility: for an explicit state, the optimal entanglement distillation rate remains strictly smaller than the entanglement cost. Thus, substantial catalytic assistance does not remove some of the fundamental limitations of mixed-state entanglement manipulation.

</details>


### [100] [Shadow models of a quantum model for cloud cover and the influence of finite sampling noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20076)
*Hedwig Keller,Mierk Schwabe,Veronika Eyring*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何通过构造性阴影模型（不经过显式训练或回归阶段）来替代量子机器学习（QML）模型，使量子资源仅在训练阶段需要，从而实现QML模型与经典气候模型的耦合。作者将经典插值方法与量子傅里叶模型的近似（即电路的部分傅里叶级数表示）进行比较，并在Euro-Q-Exa量子系统上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算硬件存在局限性，量子机器学习模型难以直接集成到经典应用中。为克服这一瓶颈，需要将QML模型转换为"阴影"（经典代理模型），使量子资源仅在训练阶段使用，从而实现量子模型与经典系统（如气候模型）的高效耦合。

Method: 采用无需显式训练或回归阶段的构造性阴影化过程。利用先前开发的云覆盖QML模型的编码策略，将量子电路表示为部分傅里叶级数，并通过离散傅里叶变换在经典端高效重建电路。对比了经典插值方法与傅里叶级数截断方法，并在Euro-Q-Exa（基于IQM Radiance超导量子比特系统）上进行了实验验证。

Result: 两种阴影化方法在特定条件下均具有缓解有限采样噪声的效果，这为超越硬件受限时代使用阴影模型提供了动机。在Euro-Q-Exa量子系统上的实验中也观察到了误差缓解效应，但尚难将其与系统校准相关的误差区分开来。

Conclusion: 构造性阴影模型是连接QML与经典应用的可行途径，且其噪声缓解效果使其不仅在当前硬件受限时期有价值，在未来量子硬件更成熟时仍有使用动机。截断傅里叶级数可进一步减小阴影模型的规模。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a quickly growing field that is promising various advantages compared to conventional computing. However, currently stand-alone quantum applications are scarce and hybrid (quantum-classical) computing is needed, especially in quantum machine learning (QML). Due to current limitations of quantum computing hardware and the coupling between HPC and quantum devices, integrating a trained (QML) model in classical applications is challenging. In this case, it is helpful to couple so-called shadows of the QML model instead, i.e., classical models that imitate the input-output relations of QML models such that quantum resources are only needed during the training stage. Here we consider constructive shadowing processes without an explicit training or regression stage to avoid rendering the QML model redundant, and apply them to a previously developed QML model for cloud cover [1] to allow for an efficient coupling to a climate model. We compare classical interpolation methods to an approximation of the quantum Fourier model, the representation of the circuit as a partial Fourier series. The encoding strategy in [1] allows the use of the discrete Fourier transform to efficiently reconstruct the circuits classically. Truncating the partial Fourier series further reduces the size of the shadow models. Both methods have the effect of mitigating finite sampling noise under certain conditions, which yields a motivation to use shadow models also beyond the era of limited hardware availability. Further, we compute the shadow models on the quantum system Euro-Q-Exa, based on the IQM Radiance system with superconducting qubits, where error mitigating effects can also be observed, albeit it is still difficult to distinguish them from errors connected to the calibration of the system.

</details>


### [101] [Squeezed- and coherent-state quantum key distribution over a deployed hybrid fibre-free-space channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20088)
*Dnan A. E. Hajomer,Huy Q. Nguyen,Ivan Derkach,Andreas B. Kidmose,Edoardo Rossi,Mattia Sabatini,Yoann Pietri,Marco Avesani,Francesco Vedovato,Michael Hentschel,Radim Filip,Giuseppe Vallone,Vladyslav Usenko,Tobias Gehring,Soren Forchhammer,Paolo Villoresi,Ulrik L. Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在由620米自由空间链路和2公里部署光纤组成的混合信道上（总损耗达20 dB），展示了基于本地生成本振的连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD），同时支持高斯调制相干态和压缩态两种协议。通过统一的适应性后处理框架（基于透射率聚类的分簇、协方差矩阵平均/去衰落的残差衰落缓解、以及速率自适应盲纠错），将信道适配从光学系统转移到后处理层，实现了相干态协议0.42 Mbit/s和压缩态协议0.93 Mbit/s的渐近密钥率，并在部署的大气信道上首次建立了压缩态CV-QKD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要将光纤与自由空间链路结合，但现有CV-QKD系统通常只针对单一介质开发。光纤传输稳定但自由空间大气传播存在透射率波动，加密安全性受损，需在短时间尺度内解决。跨光纤-自由空间串联混合信道上的CV-QKD运作尚属空白，因此需要在混合信道上实现安全、高效的密钥分发。

Method: 采用本地生成本振（locally generated LO）的CV-QKD方案，避免传输本振的干扰。提出了统一的适应性后处理框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于透射率聚类的信道分簇；(2) 通过协方差矩阵平均或去衰落（de-fading）来缓解残余透射率波动；(3) 速率自适应盲纠错（rate-adaptive blind reconciliation）。该框架同时适用于相干态和压缩态两种协议，并针对两种协议不同的安全分析和统计需求进行了适配。

Result: 在总损耗20 dB的部署混合信道（620米自由空间+2公里光纤）上，相干态协议实现0.42 Mbit/s、压缩态协议实现0.93 Mbit/s的渐近密钥率。自适应后处理框架中的速率自适应盲纠错单独恢复了多达19%的额外密钥。首次在部署的大气信道上实现了压缩态CV-QKD。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对传输介质的适配可以大幅转移到数据处理层，无需针对每种介质改造光学系统。这为构建跨越光纤、地面自由空间和卫星链路的异构量子网络提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks will combine optical fibre with free-space links, yet continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) has been developed predominantly for one medium or the other, while operation across concatenated fibre-free-space channels remains largely unexplored. The two media impose contrasting requirements: fibre transmission is stable and permits long processing intervals, whereas atmospheric propagation imposes transmittance fluctuations that degrade security and must be resolved on short timescales. Here we demonstrate a locally generated local oscillator CV-QKD with both Gaussian-modulated coherent and squeezed states over a deployed hybrid channel comprising a 620-m free-space link and 2 km of deployed fibre, with a total loss up to 20 dB. Rather than adapting the optics to each medium, we move channel adaptation to the post-processing, through a unified adaptive post-processing framework coupling transmittance-based clustering, residual-fading mitigation by covariance-matrix averaging or de-fading, and rate-adaptive blind reconciliation, which alone recovers up to 19% additional key. The same adaptive-processing principle is applied to both protocols, while accounting for their different security analyses and statistical requirements, yielding asymptotic secret-key rates of 0.42 Mbit per sec for the coherent-state protocol and 0.93 Mbit per sec for the squeezed-state protocol under the respective channel conditions, and establishing squeezed-state CV-QKD over a deployed atmospheric channel. These results show that adaptation to the transmission medium can largely be transferred to the data-processing layer, providing a route towards heterogeneous quantum networks spanning fibre, terrestrial free-space and satellite links.

</details>


### [102] [Counterexamples to the fractional coloring conjecture for triply efficient shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20113)
*Jędrzej Stempin,Santiago Llorens,Felix Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一族量子态和泡利算符集，证明了King等人提出的猜想13（关于Pauli观测量的反交换图的分数色数χ_f满足χ_f·ε²≤C的界）是错误的，即不存在满足该有界不等式的常数C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数图着色在Pauli观测量的阴影层析中具有重要应用。若猜想13成立，则意味着对任意Pauli算子子集S存在三重高效的Pauli阴影层析算法。因此验证或反驳该猜想对量子态层析算法的理论保证至关重要。

Method: 构造一族量子态和可观测量的反例族，证明不存在有限常数C满足χ_f·ε²≤C。进一步利用图的交换指标（commutation index）或β数给出更一般的构造。核心证明工具是放大技巧，通过字典序图积（lexicographic graph products）对分数色数、β数和期望值进行放大。

Result: 成功构造了反例族，证明猜想13不成立：不存在有限常数C使得对所有量子态和反交换图G，χ_f·ε²≤C恒成立。同时给出基于β数的更一般构造，说明该界在其他图参数下同样被破坏。

Conclusion: 猜想13被证伪，意味着不能仅依赖该猜想获得对任意Pauli算子子集S的三重高效阴影层析算法的通用保证。未来需要寻找更精细的图结构条件或替代的不变量来刻画可高效层析的Pauli算符集。

Abstract: Fractional graph colorings are useful for the Shadow tomography of Pauli observables. In practice, it is desirable that any experimentally interesting set of Pauli operators has a small fractional chromatic number $χ_{f}$ for its anticommutation graph. Conjecture 13 in King, Gosset, Kothari, and Babbush [PRX Quantum 6, 010336 (2025)] states that if $B_ε(\varrho)$ is the set of Pauli observables having expectation value magnitude at least $ε$ in some given quantum state $\varrho$, then the fractional chromatic number of the anticommutation graph $G$ induced by $B_ε(\varrho)$ is $O(ε^{-2})$. In other words, it asserts that there exists a constant $C$ such that $χ_{f} \cdot ε^2 \leq C$ on all states and graphs. If the conjecture were true, it would imply that there exists a triply efficient Pauli shadow tomography algorithm for {\it any} subset $S$ of Pauli observables, provided that there is also an efficient fractional coloring algorithm for the set $B_ε$. Here we show that the conjecture is false by constructing a family of states and observables for which no finite $C$ satisfying the bound exists. We also give a more general construction relying on the commutation index or $β$ number of a graph. The key ingredient in the proofs can be seen as an instance of the amplification trick, where fractional chromatic numbers, $β$ numbers, and expectation values are amplified through lexicographic graph products.

</details>


### [103] [SAKE: Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion for Geometric Liouvillian Transport. A Differential-Geometric Framework for Response Transport in Quantum Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20132)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuna,Hao Li,Simon Paiva-Ortega*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出光谱自动微分核展开（SAKE），一种可微分的计算框架，用于在相邻量子动力学模型之间传输非线性光谱响应。通过前向模式自动微分与Duhamel传输理论结合，构建围绕参考模型的局部传输展开，避免为每个哈密顿量或Liouvillian独立重算多维光谱。在具有su(2)×su(2)对称性的四能级激子二聚体上验证，三阶展开能准确复现投影传输算子及其路径混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非线性光谱计算方法需要对每个不同的哈密顿量或Liouvillian分别重新计算多维光谱，计算成本高昂且不利于参数探索、灵敏度分析和逆向设计。作者旨在开发一个可微分的框架，使得模型参数微扰后无需完全重算光谱，而是通过数学传输算子在模型间高效迁移响应。

Method: SAKE框架的核心是将前向模式自动微分与Duhamel传输理论相结合。具体而言：1）利用自动微分生成参数依赖Liouvillian的一阶、二阶和三阶导数；2）将这些导数组装成路径传输算子（pathway transport operator）；3）该算子将参考模型的非线性响应映射到相邻系统的响应上。验证中比较二、三阶传输路径算子与直接计算得到的精确投影传输矩阵。

Result: 在四能级激子二聚体（具有su(2)×su(2)对称性）上，三阶展开能够准确复现投影传输算子及其相关的路径混合。此外，传输算子揭示了相干和耗散扰动如何在双面Feynman路径之间重新分配振幅，暴露了非线性光谱中不直接可见的机制信息。

Conclusion: SAKE建立了一个用于非线性光谱的可微分计算框架，支持高效的局部参数探索、灵敏度分析和未来的逆向设计应用。相比传统逐点重算方法，该框架不仅更高效，还能提供额外机制层面的洞察。

Abstract: We introduce the Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion (SAKE), a differentiable computational framework for transporting nonlinear spectroscopic response between neighboring quantum dynamical models. Rather than recomputing multidimensional spectra independently for each Hamiltonian or Liouvillian, SAKE constructs local transport expansions about a reference model by combining forward-mode automatic differentiation with Duhamel transport theory. Automatic differentiation generates first-, second-, and third-order derivatives of the parameter-dependent Liouvillian, which are assembled into a pathway transport operator that maps the nonlinear response of a reference model onto neighboring systems. The framework is validated for a four-level excitonic dimer possessing an $su(2)\times su(2)$ symmetry by comparing second- and third-order transported pathway operators with exact projected transport matrices obtained from direct calculations. The third-order expansion accurately reproduces the projected transport operator and its associated pathway mixing. Beyond providing an efficient computational strategy, the transport operator reveals how coherent and dissipative perturbations redistribute amplitude among double-sided Feynman pathways, exposing mechanistic information that is not directly apparent from the nonlinear spectrum. SAKE thereby establishes a differentiable computational framework for nonlinear spectroscopy that supports efficient local parameter exploration, sensitivity analysis, and future inverse-design applications.

</details>


### [104] [Preparation of Large Fock States in Resonators with High Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20133)
*Lucas R. S. Santos,Ciro M. Diniz,Daniel Z. Rossatto,Celso J. Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合量子非破坏（QND）光子数编码与量子振幅放大（QAA）的协议，实现了高概率、可预示的产生大Fock态，在高达八个量子比特的寄存器下可近确定性制备数百激发数的Fock态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Fock态是玻色子量子信息和量子增强计量中的重要资源，但其高激发数下的高概率制备仍具挑战性：确定性方法需要大量控制资源，而基于测量的方法通常预示概率很低。

Method: 该协议从腔模的相干态出发，利用量子相位估计（QPE）将光子数信息编码到多量子比特寄存器中，再通过量子振幅放大（QAA）在测量前提升目标结果的概率。该方案可在色散电路QED中直接实现，也可适配具有QND光子数读出的其他玻色子平台（如腔QED）。

Result: 在最多八比特的寄存器下，可近确定性制备具有数百激发数的Fock态。此外，该协议还可作为首级模块，通过条件分束器操作扩展生成双模NOON态。

Conclusion: 该方案有效克服了大Fock态制备中确定性方法与测量方法各自的瓶颈，实现了高预示概率的大Fock态生成，并具备向NOON态扩展的潜力，为玻色子量子信息处理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Large Fock states are important resources for bosonic quantum information and quantum-enhanced metrology, but preparing them with high probability at large excitation numbers remains challenging, as deterministic methods become increasingly control-intensive, while measurement-based approaches typically suffer from low heralding probabilities. Here we propose a protocol that combines quantum nondemolition photon-number encoding with quantum amplitude amplification to enable high-probability heralded generation of large Fock states. Starting from a cavity mode prepared in a coherent state, Quantum Phase Estimation encodes photon-number information into a multi-qubit register, while Quantum Amplitude Amplification boosts the probability of a desired target outcome before measurement. The scheme has an immediate implementation in dispersive circuit-QED, but can be analogously adapted to other bosonic platforms with QND photon-number readout, such as cavity-QED. With a register of up to eight qubits, near-deterministic preparation of Fock states with hundreds of excitations is possible. We also show that the protocol can serve as the first stage of an extension toward generating a two-mode NOON state via a conditional beam-splitter operation.

</details>


### [105] [Vacuum viscosity and relativistic inertia: Motion of a massive object with charged internal degrees of freedom interacting with a classical field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20140)
*Jen-Tsung Hsiang,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个带有内部自由度的运动大质量物体与自由量子场相互作用的微观模型，探讨其是否受到真空黏性力（即"量子摩擦"）的影响。非相对论计算给出肯定答案，但相对论协变计算给出否定答案，作者指出非相对论框架的缺陷所在，并由此丰富了牛顿第一、第二定律在狭义相对论约束下的内涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受两类经典问题启发：一是自70年代以来研究的宇宙粒子产生及相关联的动态Casimir效应，二是中性原子沿介电表面运动时经历的量子摩擦。在宇宙膨胀或移动镜子的场景中，产生粒子的反作用效应可以通过真空黏性（源于量子场涨落）来理解。作者试图追问这一效应的起源：一个带有内部电荷自由度的大质量物体与自由无界限的经典标量场在零温下相互作用时，是否也会像前述先例一样感受到黏性力。

Method: 采用一个用于光力学（optomechanics）的微物理模型，该模型能够处理χ-φ-M三方不等权相互作用。作者首先进行了非相对论计算（鉴于原子物理的需求，这看似完全合理），然后进行了相对论协变计算以作对照，并对比两者的结果。

Result: 非相对论计算给出的答案是"是"——存在黏性力；但相对论协变计算给出的答案是"否"——不存在该力。作者进一步定位了非相对论框架中存在的缺陷，并指出这一潜在却真实的冲突的解决在技术上具有非平凡性。

Conclusion: 解决这一冲突不仅在物理上具有启发性，还使得牛顿第一定律和第二定律在狭义相对论原则被强制施加时获得更丰富的内容，同时给出了在非相对论框架下获得正确答案所应遵循的规则。

Abstract: Our present investigation into a rather rudimentary problem is motivated by two classes of problems studied since the 70's, cosmological particle creation and its more accessible analog, the dynamical Casimir effect on the one hand, and quantum friction a neutral atom moving along a dielectric surface would experience, on the other. The backreaction effects of produced particles being able to isotropize the expansion of the universe, or to slow down the moving mirror can be understood via the concept of vacuum viscosity arising from fluctuations of the quantum field. We want to track down the origin of this effect by asking the question whether a moving massive $M$ object with a charged internal degrees of freedom $χ$ interacting with a free unbounded classical field $φ$ at zero temperature would experience a viscous force, similar to the said precedents. Adopting a microphysics model for optomechanics which can treat the unequal tripartite $χ$-$φ$-$M$ interactions, we first perform a nonrelativistic calculation, which seems perfectly legitimate considering the needs of atomic physics, and found the answer to be yes, but a relativistic covariant calculation says no. We identify where the nonrelativistic framework is defective. The resolution of this latent yet real conflict is technically nontrivial but physically quite inspirational. It results in added enriched contents to Newton's first and second laws when the principles of special relativity are enforced, and rules to follow to get the correct nonrelativistic answer

</details>


### [106] [Proper Learning of Shallow All-to-All Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20162)
*Steven Kordonowy,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究浅层量子电路学习问题的一种变体：在已知电路门布局的前提下，通过迭代式的局部门逆传来学习具体使用的门，从而构建操作等价的电路。作者提出了一个元算法框架，并将其应用于随机全连接二局域电路，通过光锥增长分析提供了该系综在深度d* ~ log2 n + log2 log2 n处发生尖锐可学习性转变的分析和数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机是解决浅层量子电路的学习问题——不仅要学习电路的整体功能，还要学习其中使用的具体量子门。这一研究对于量子密码学方案有直接影响，因为这类方案的安全性依赖于电路学习困难性。理解电路可学习性的边界条件对于评估和设计此类量子加密协议至关重要。

Method: 本文提出一个元算法框架，其核心思想是在电路前端和后端进行迭代式的局部门逆传操作。具体来说，从已知的Haar随机砖墙电路学习工作出发，将其推广到更广泛的电路类别。作者将该技术应用于随机全连接二局域电路，并通过光锥增长（lightcone growth）分析来刻画该系综的可学习性转变。

Result: 作者提供了解析和数值两方面的证据，表明随机全连接二局域电路系综在深度d* ~ log2 n + log2 log2 n（在大尺寸极限下）处经历一个尖锐的可学习性转变。这一结论基于光锥增长分析得出。

Conclusion: 这些结果对近期提出的基于电路学习困难性的量子密码学方案具有潜在影响，但本文设置与那些方案之间存在重要区别，因此指出了未来研究的可能方向。

Abstract: This work considers a variation on the problem of learning shallow quantum circuits. Given query access to the circuit, as well as knowledge of its gate layout, we consider the task of learning the specific gates used in the circuit, producing an operationally-equivalent circuit matching this structure. Building on recent work for learning Haar random brickwork circuits, we identify a meta-algorithmic framework for learning broad classes of circuits based on iterative local gate inversions at the front and back of the circuit. We apply these techniques to study random, all-to-all, two-local circuits, and provide analytical and numerical evidence that this ensemble undergoes a sharp learnability transition at depth $d^* \sim \log_2 n + \log_2\log_2 n$ in the large size limit, based on an analysis of lightcone growth. These results have implications for recently proposed quantum cryptographic schemes based on the difficulty of circuit learning, though there are important distinctions with respect to our setting that suggest avenues for future study.

</details>


### [107] [Architecture and Compilation Co-Design for High-Rate Quantum Product Codes on Neutral Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20164)
*Adrian Liu,Wan-Hsuan Lin,Daniel Bochen Tan,Qian Xu,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ONEX框架，用于解决qLDPC码在原子阵列平台上物理执行规划的编译瓶颈问题。通过将复杂2D执行规划分解为可独立求解的1D子问题，利用SMT编码实现深度最优的方案，并在超图乘积码上实现3.7-6.1倍（对比构造性1D算法）和29.8-42.1倍（对比通用2D编译器）的时钟速率提升，可扩展至2500数据量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用规模的容错量子计算需要高编码率的量子纠错码，qLDPC码是重要候选。然而合成有效且高效的物理执行计划是公认的困难组合问题，形成编译瓶颈且随码规模增大而恶化，亟需高效的编译框架来克服这一复杂性。

Method: ONEX框架包含三大核心：1) 将2D物理执行规划分解为独立1D子问题，并通过可满足性模理论（SMT）显式编码1D执行计划，产生可证明深度最优的解决方案；2) 多阶段编译流水线，包含随时优化（anytime optimization）、移动压缩（movement compaction）和迭代反馈，在保持实际墙钟时间的同时提供渐进式优化和按需获取高质量方案；3) 扩展到分区布局（zoned layouts）和更广泛的提升积（LP）码族。

Result: 在映射到中性原子阵列的超图乘积（HGP）码存储器应用中，ONEX相比构造性1D算法提升3.7-6.1倍时钟速率，相比通用2D编译器提升29.8-42.1倍，并能高效扩展到2500个数据量子比特的码规模。同时揭示了分区布局中的架构权衡，并通过代表性示例展示了在提升积（LP）码上的适用性。

Conclusion: ONEX通过维度缩减策略将复杂的硬组合编译问题转化为可高效求解的子问题，在保证深度最优性的同时大幅提升编译效率和执行性能，为qLDPC码在中性原子阵列平台上的实用化铺平了道路，并具备扩展到更多码族与布局的潜力。

Abstract: Achieving fault-tolerant quantum computing at a practical scale demands quantum error correction (QEC) codes with high encoding rates. Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes emerge as a promising candidate, especially given the rise of neutral atom arrays that provide dynamic long-range connectivity via atom movements. In general, synthesizing valid and efficient physical execution plans for QEC is a provably hard combinatorial problem, forming a critical compilation bottleneck that worsens as code sizes grow. To overcome this complexity, we focus on an important product family of qLDPC codes with dimension-reduction properties, and propose ONEX. This framework decomposes complex 2D physical execution planning into independent 1D subproblems, each solved to optimal execution depth within practical compilation time. First, we formulate the 1D execution plan with an explicit satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) encoding. This protocol produces provably depth-optimal solutions with substantial duration reduction. Second, we develop a multi-stage compilation pipeline featuring anytime optimization, movement compaction, and iterative feedback. This pipeline maintains practical wall-clock times while providing progressive refinement and on-demand retrieval of quality solutions. Third, we evaluate ONEX in the application of hypergraph product (HGP) code memory mapped onto neutral atom arrays, achieving 3.7x to 6.1x and 29.8x to 42.1x higher clock rates than the constructive 1D algorithm and the general 2D compiler, respectively, while scaling efficiently to codes with 2,500 data qubits. Finally, we extend ONEX to zoned layouts, revealing architectural insights into the associated trade-offs, and demonstrate its applicability to the broader lifted-product (LP) code family through a representative example.

</details>


### [108] [Large Scale Entanglement Structure Detection in 100-Qubit Systems via Local Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20170)
*Rui Li,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Shikun Zhang,Zheng Qin,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Zhisong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的框架，通过局部关联指纹直接识别多体量子态的纠缠结构（即态如何分成未纠缠的块），通过选择满足边界匹配条件p_1 = p_R的局域泡利基，在单一测量配置下读出整个链，测量开销与系统大小无关。在含噪模拟中，该单基协议对多达100个量子比特的30个候选分区中GHZ型、W型和团簇型结构的分类平均准确率超过95%，并在超导量子处理器上验证了最多13个量子比特的可靠分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体量子态的纠缠结构是量子信息科学的核心任务，但传统量子层析方法的开销随系统规模呈指数增长，无法扩展到大规模系统，因此迫切需要一种可扩展的纠缠结构识别框架。

Method: 提出一种可扩展框架，从局部关联指纹（local correlation fingerprints）直接识别大规模纠缠结构。通过选择满足边界匹配条件p_1 = p_R的代表性局域泡利基，使整个链在单一测量配置下完成读出，从而将测量开销与系统大小解耦。协议在含噪模拟和超导量子处理器上均得到验证。

Result: 在含噪模拟中，单基协议对最多100量子比特系统中30个候选分区的GHZ型、W型和团簇型纠缠结构分类平均准确率超过95%；在超导量子处理器上，对最多13量子比特的块结构分类可靠，更大尺寸时因噪声和深度引起的退化而失效。

Conclusion: 通过显式映射这些失效模式，研究界定了硬件级可扩展的边界，并为在近期量子设备上刻画纠缠结构提供了具体策略。

Abstract: Identifying the entanglement structure of a many-body quantum state, namely how its constituents partition into unentangled blocks, is a central task in quantum information science, yet conventional tomography scales exponentially with system size. Here we introduce a scalable framework that recognizes large-scale entanglement structures directly from local correlation fingerprints. By choosing a representative local Pauli basis that satisfies a boundary-matching condition p_1 = p_R, the entire chain is read out in a single measurement configuration, keeping the measurement effort independent of system size. In noisy simulations, this single-basis protocol classifies GHZ-, W-, and cluster-type structures among 30 candidate partitions with a mean accuracy exceeding 95% for systems of up to 100 qubits. We further validate the protocol on a superconducting quantum processor, where it reliably classifies block structures for systems of up to 13 qubits before noise- and depth-induced degradation sets in at larger sizes. By mapping these failure modes explicitly, our results delineate the boundary of hardware-level scalability and point to a concrete strategy for characterizing entanglement structure on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [109] [Characterization of a damping channel as a mixture of amplitude damping and anti-damping channels of different parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20178)
*Vijay Pathak,R. Srikanth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过混合具有不等衰减参数和依赖于时间的混合概率的振幅阻尼与反振幅阻尼通道，来生成更广泛类别的相位协变量子动力学（包括非幺正和幺正）的构造性框架，并利用P-可分割性一般定理在适当选择的希尔伯特空间基下进行表征，实现了对收缩和平移的独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非幺正噪声是开放量子系统的基本特征，导致布洛赫球上的状态平移。虽然平移对态制备和信道容量有用，但量子信息处理（特别是纠错）通常更偏好无翻译的幺正动力学。现有模型如广义振幅阻尼（GAD）通道对耗散、激发和退相位过程的控制能力有限，缺乏更灵活的参数调节手段。

Method: 通过混合振幅阻尼与反振幅阻尼通道，利用不等的衰减参数和依赖于时间的混合概率来构造更广泛的相位协变动力学。采用P-可分割性的一般定理，在适当选择的希尔伯特空间基下对其进行评估，简化了幺正和非幺正情况下的条件，从而能对动力学进行分类（CP-可分割、P-可分割但非CP-可分割、以及非P-可分割）。

Result: 该框架实现了对收缩和平移的独立控制，允许在非幺正和幺正区间之间连续调谐，并产生了GAD中不存在的有效退相位贡献。框架能覆盖包括CP-可分割、P-可分割但非CP-可分割、以及非P-可分割等多种动力学行为。适当的混合可以减少与理想无噪声演化的偏差，且这种改进即使在调谐到幺正区间后仍然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为超越标准热模型的开放量子系统动力学工程提供了一种灵活方法，在噪声控制和量子信息处理方面具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Non-unital noise is a fundamental feature of open quantum systems, governing energy exchange and inducing state translations on the Bloch sphere. While such translations can be useful for tasks such as state preparation and channel capacity, unital dynamics where no translation occurs are often preferred in quantum information processing, particularly for error correction. Phase-covariant dynamics provide a general framework encompassing dissipation, excitation, and dephasing processes in qubit systems; however, commonly used models such as the generalized amplitude damping (GAD) channel offer only limited control over these features. In this work, we present a constructive framework for generating a broader class of phase-covariant dynamics by mixing amplitude-damping and anti-damping channels with unequal decay parameters and time-dependent mixing probabilities. This approach enables independent control over contraction and translation, allows continuous tuning between non-unital and unital regimes, and yields an effective dephasing contribution absent in GAD. To characterize these dynamics, we employ the general theorem for P-divisibility and show that it can be used constructively by evaluating it in suitably chosen Hilbert space bases, leading to simplified conditions for both unital and non-unital cases. The framework captures a wide range of behaviors, including CP-divisible, P-divisible but not CP-divisible, and non-P-divisible dynamics. We further show that appropriate mixing can reduce the deviation from the ideal noiseless evolution and that such improvement persists even after tuning the dynamics to the unital regime. Our results provide a flexible approach to engineering open system dynamics beyond standard thermal models, with potential applications in noise control and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [110] [Hybrid Qubit-Rotor Quantum Systems: Clifford Structure, Universal Control, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20227)
*Dengyao Luo,Arvin Kushwaha,Mastawal Tirfe,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了n个量子比特与r个量子转子混合寄存器的Clifford理论，分类了保持Weyl对易关系的混合相空间自同构并给出每个自同构的有限Clifford电路。结果表明转子动量奇偶性可以控制量子比特Pauli操作（属于Clifford群），而每个非零的量子比特受控转子动量移位都是非Clifford的。文中还给出了混合耦合的范式和最小合成门数，并证明在Clifford局域操作基础上添加转子余弦势和一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位可在强算子拓扑下实现通用控制。该结构应用于规范协变物质跳跃的非Clifford受控移位实现、具有动量支撑和能量约束的转子相位估计，以及转子动量码上的有限傅里叶变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U(1)量子转子将周期角度与整数值共轭动量配对，出现在分子转动、超导相位-电荷电路和紧致规范场中。将转子与量子比特相干耦合可形成混合寄存器，但其控制结构既不同于振子-量子比特情形，也不同于qudit情形。此前缺乏针对这种混合系统的系统化Clifford理论和通用控制结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者建立了n量子比特与r转子的混合相空间F_2^{2n}×Z^r×T^r，分类所有保持Weyl对易关系的自同构，并为每个自同构给出显式有限Clifford电路。分类揭示了方向性：转子动量奇偶性可控制量子比特Pauli操作（Clifford），而量子比特受控转子动量移位是非Clifford的。文中还推导了混合耦合的范式及其最小合成门数，并利用转子余弦势和固定的量子比特-转子条件相位证明强算子拓扑下的通用性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1)混合相位空间自同构的完整分类及对应的有限Clifford电路；(2)混合qubit-rotor耦合的范式和精确最小合成门数；(3)局域Clifford操作加上转子余弦势和一个固定条件相位可给出强算子拓扑下的通用控制；(4)三个应用：规范协变物质跳跃的受控移位实现（必为非Clifford）、具有动量/能量约束的转子相位估计与探针优化、以及转子动量码上的有限傅里叶变换——其中d=2^s的单转子变换编译为O(s)个动量选择和受控移位指令，每个跨寄存器傅里叶因子由一个二次转子Clifford门实现。

Conclusion: 本文为量子比特-转子混合系统建立了完整的Clifford理论框架，阐明了其与纯量子比特或纯转子情形的本质区别，并展示了该理论在规范理论模拟、相位估计和量子傅里叶变换等方向的直接应用价值。

Abstract: A $U(1)$ quantum rotor pairs a periodic angle with an integer-valued conjugate momentum, and occurs in molecular rotation, superconducting phase-charge circuits, and compact gauge fields. Coupling such a rotor coherently to qubits gives a hybrid register whose control structure is not inherited from either the oscillator-qubit or the qudit case. We develop a Clifford theory, together with a universal-control result, for registers of $n$ qubits and $r$ rotors. We classify all automorphisms of the hybrid phase space $\mathbb{F}_2^{2n}\times\mathbb{Z}^r\times\mathbb{T}^r$ that preserve the Weyl commutation relations, and give an explicit finite Clifford circuit for each one. The classification is directional: rotor momentum parity may control qubit Pauli operations within the Clifford group, while every nonzero qubit-controlled rotor momentum shift is non-Clifford. It also yields normal forms for the mixed qubit-rotor couplings and the exact minimum number of elementary mixed gates needed to synthesize them. Adding a rotor cosine potential and one fixed qubit-rotor conditional phase to the local Clifford operations gives universal control on the full Hilbert space in the strong operator topology. We then apply this structure in three settings: an exact controlled-shift realization of gauge-covariant matter hopping, which is necessarily non-Clifford; rotor phase estimation with direct angle readout and probe optimization under momentum-support and energy constraints; and finite Fourier transforms on rotor momentum codes, where the one-rotor transform for $d=2^s$ compiles into $O(s)$ momentum-selective and controlled-shift instructions and each cross-register Fourier factor is implemented by one quadratic rotor Clifford gate.

</details>


### [111] [Jamming Extensions of Quantum Correlations Lead to Hidden Superluminal Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20247)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan,Xie Sicheng,Michał Eckstein,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了相对论因果性（RC）与量子关联之间的关系，证明了任何非平凡的jamming效应——无论是态无关还是态相关——都会导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。因此，只有两种一致的可能性：无jamming的标准量子关联，或完整的相对论因果关联集合。此外，文章展示了在jamming几何中，RC攻击可以破解设备无关（DI）比特承诺和秘密共享协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特定时空构型下，相对论因果性允许"jamming"现象——一种超光速因果影响但不构成超光速信号传递。这引出了一个关键问题：在量子态下，jamming是否可以与相对论因果性共存而不产生隐藏的信号传递？同时，现有设备无关（DI）密码学原语的安全性通常只在no-signaling或量子对手下被分析，缺乏对仅受相对论因果性约束的对手的分析。

Method: 作者通过扩展PRL 104, 140404中的算子框架来完整刻画与相对论因果性（RC）相容的关联集合。在要求底层态为量子态的条件下，证明了任何非平凡的jamming（不论态无关还是态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。利用这一刻画，构造了针对DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享协议的显式RC攻击。

Result: 证明了与量子态相容的、满足相对论因果性的关联要么是标准量子关联（无jamming），要么是完整的RC关联集合（包含jamming），中间态（量子关联加jamming扩展）必然导致可观测的信号传递。在jamming几何中构造了具体的RC攻击，能够攻破DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享协议，而这些协议在no-signaling对手下是安全的。

Conclusion: 安全性的保证需要行为（behaviors）与测量事件在时空中的位置信息共同指定；仅靠输入输出统计量不足以确保对相对论因果性对手的安全性。这对量子密码学的安全模型提出了重要修正。

Abstract: Relativistic causality in certain spacetime configurations permits jamming - superluminal causal influences that nevertheless do not enable superluminal signaling. We fully characterize the correlations compatible with relativistic causality (RC) by extending the operator framework of PRL 104, 140404. When the underlying state is required to be quantum, we prove that any nontrivial jamming - whether state-independent or state-dependent - necessarily leads to hidden superluminal signaling. Thus, the only consistent possibilities are standard quantum correlations without jamming or the full relativistically causal correlation set, with intermediate jamming extensions of quantum correlations leading to signaling. As an application of our characterization, we investigate the security of device-independent (DI) cryptographic primitives against adversaries constrained only by relativistic causality. We construct explicit RC attacks that break DI bit commitment and DI secret sharing in jamming geometries, even when these protocols remain secure against no-signaling adversaries. Our results show that security against relativistic adversaries requires behaviors to be specified together with the spacetime locations of their measurement events; input-output statistics alone are insufficient.

</details>


### [112] [Logarithmic depth compression of Heisenberg Hamiltonian simulation by fan-out parallelization, with built-in error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20250)
*Artemiy Burov,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于fan-out的gadget编译器，通过将每个逻辑自旋编码到按其交互度大小确定的小型重复码寄存器中，用电路宽度置换电路深度，从而在NISQ设备上更高效地模拟Heisenberg型NMR哈密顿量。在13自旋演示中深度减半、体积降低1.7倍，但对均匀稠密图性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ量子计算机受限于电路深度，而自旋系统的product-formula模拟通常产生窄而深的电路，这严重限制了可模拟系统的规模。需要在现有硬件约束下找到更优的编译策略。

Method: 提出fan-out-based gadget编译器：将每个逻辑自旋编码到由交互度决定大小的重复码寄存器中，使同种Pauli类型的所有成对交互在对数深度CNOT fan-out后并行执行，冗余寄存器提供用于post-selection的检错能力且不增加额外算法开销。在heavy-hex超导和全连接trapped-ion两种目标架构上进行固定协议的资源对比。

Result: 对于具有高度枢纽节点的交互图，体积最优调度使双量子比特深度减半，13自旋演示的体积降低1.7倍；在全连接架构上最高度数分子的深度降低达2.5倍；全连接下所有系统的双量子比特门数均增加，体积降低仅在深度受限硬件上有益。增益随图的度数不均匀性增长，在均匀稠密图上退化。基于真实噪声模型的模拟表明，gadget电路仅在利用内置检错进行post-selection后才能匹敌或超越顺序编译，且需要误差率改善1到1.5个数量级。

Conclusion: fan-out-based gadget编译器是深度受限NISQ硬件上模拟NMR自旋系统的有效策略，尤其适用于交互图具有高度不均匀性的系统，但当前噪声水平下需结合post-selection检错才能体现优势，且需要误差率降低一个数量级以上。

Abstract: Noisy intermediate-scale quantum computers are constrained by circuit depth, while product-formula simulation of spin systems leads to narrow and deep circuits. Here we introduce a fan-out-based gadget compiler that trades circuit depth for width in simulations of Heisenberg-type nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) Hamiltonians. Each logical spin is encoded into a small repetition-code register sized by its interaction degree, so that all pairwise interactions of a given Pauli type execute in parallel after a logarithmic-depth CNOT fan-out, and the redundant registers provide error detection for post-selection at no additional algorithmic overhead. The central result is a fixed-protocol resource comparison of the two compilations, transpiled to heavy-hex superconducting and all-to-all trapped-ion targets across a set of NMR spin systems. For interaction graphs with a high-degree hub the volume-optimal schedule halves the two-qubit depth and reduces the volume 1.7-fold for the 13-spin demonstration, which on heavy-hex also lowers the two-qubit gate count, and the depth reduction rises to 2.5-fold on all-to-all for the highest-degree molecule studied. On all-to-all the two-qubit gate count rises for every system, so the volume reduction is a benefit on depth-limited hardware. The gain grows with the degree inhomogeneity of the interaction graph and vanishes for dense uniform graphs, where the optimum is the sequential circuit. We simulate the zero-field NMR spectrum of tetramethylsilane, a 13-spin star system. Under a noise model scaled from a published present-day processor calibration, the shallower gadget circuits match or surpass the sequential compilation only after post-selection on their built-in error detection, once error rates improve by one to one and a half orders of magnitude. We verify the spectra against an independent classical computation.

</details>


### [113] [Analytical Solutions of the Generalized Klein-Gordon Oscillator in Som-Raychaudhuri Space-Time via the Extended Nikiforov-Uvarov Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20273)
*Hale Karayer,Tolga Celik,Dogan Demirhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法，在Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下求解了均匀磁场中广义Klein-Gordon谐振子问题，针对线性和Cornell两种势模型获得了精确的本征态解，并给出了能量本征值及对应的本征函数解析和图形分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（Som-Raychaudhuri时空）中研究Klein-Gordon谐振子在磁场作用下的量子动力学行为，是量子场论与广义相对论交叉领域的重要课题。该研究旨在扩展Klein-Gordon振荡器的可解性范围，探索不同势模型下的精确解，以深化对弯曲时空中相对论性量子系统的理解。

Method: 采用扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov（NU）方法这一数学工具，构建Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下的广义Klein-Gordon振荡器方程，引入均匀磁场，分别对线性和Cornell两种势模型进行求解。通过该方法将二阶微分方程转化为超几何型方程，从而精确求解能量本征值和本征函数。

Result: 成功获得了线性和Cornell两种势模型下的精确本征态解，确定了相应的能量本征值表达式，并对本征函数进行了系统的解析推导和图形化展示，揭示了磁场和时空几何对量子态的具体影响。

Conclusion: 扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法是求解弯曲时空中相对论性量子系统（如Som-Raychaudhuri时空中的Klein-Gordon振荡器）的有效分析工具，能够为不同势模型提供精确的量子解，为该类系统的进一步理论研究提供了基础。

Abstract: In this study, we present an analytical approach based on the extended Nikiforov-Uvarov method to solve the generalized Klein-Gordon oscillator in the presence of a uniform magnetic field within the Som-Raychaudhuri space-time. Exact eigenstate solutions are obtained for two distinct potential models, namely the linear potential and the Cornell potential. The corresponding energy eigenvalues are determined, and the associated eigenfunctions are analyzed and illustrated graphically.

</details>


### [114] [Parallel Quantum Advantage with Limited Adaptivity Requires Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20297)
*Qipeng Liu,Saachi Mutreja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Aaronson-Ambainis猜想在量子算法使用大规模并行量子查询的情形下成立：对于T次并行量子查询算法，其接受概率可以用poly(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)次经典查询在(1-δ)比例的输入上近似到ε精度。文中通过耦合定理将均匀分布预言机与"稠密分布"预言机联系起来，并进一步推广到有界前缀加并行查询、混合算法以及常数轮自适应等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson和Ambainis猜想量子查询算法的接受概率可以被几乎所有输入上的高效经典模拟所近似，这暗示指数级量子加速仅存在于具有充分结构化输入的场景中。验证该猜想有助于理解量子计算能力的边界。

Method: 证明并行查询量子算法无法区分均匀预言机分布与"稠密分布"预言机，核心技术贡献是建立联系这两类分布的耦合定理。以此为基础，逐步扩展到有界量子前缀加并行量子阶段、混合自适应经典查询后接大规模并行量子查询，以及常数轮自适应量子算法。

Result: 在并行量子查询设置下证明了Aaronson-Ambainis猜想，并进一步推广该结果到有界前缀、混合自适应经典查询以及常数轮自适应等更广泛的算法类别。与Yamakawa-Zhandry结果形成对比，说明并行查询在采样问题中仍可保持指数级量子优势。

Conclusion: 本文在并行量子查询及若干扩展场景下证明了Aaronson-Ambainis猜想，为理解量子查询算法与经典模拟之间的边界提供了重要进展，同时也厘清了并行量子查询在采样与判别问题中的能力区别。

Abstract: Aaronson and Ambainis (Theory of Computing, 2014) conjectured that quantum query algorithms admit efficient almost-everywhere classical simulation: for any $T$-query quantum algorithm, its acceptance probability can be approximated on a $(1-δ)$ fraction of inputs, up to $ε$ additive error, using $\mathrm{poly}(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)$ classical queries. At a high level, the conjecture suggests that exponential quantum speedups are possible only on sufficiently structured inputs.
  In this work, we make progress on this conjecture by proving it for quantum algorithms that make massively parallel quantum queries. In contrast, Yamakawa and Zhandry (Journal of the ACM, 2024) showed that quantum algorithms restricted to parallel queries can still achieve exponential speedups over classical algorithms for sampling problems. We establish our simulation theorem by proving the stronger statement that parallel-query quantum algorithms cannot distinguish the uniform distribution over oracles from oracles drawn from so-called "dense distributions". Our main technical contribution is a coupling theorem that relates the uniform distribution over oracles to oracles drawn from dense distributions.
  We further extend this approach beyond the purely parallel setting, obtaining simulation theorems both for algorithms with a bounded quantum-query prefix followed by a massively parallel quantum-query stage, and for hybrid algorithms that make an arbitrary polynomial number of adaptive classical queries before the massively parallel quantum-query stage. Finally, using the parallel-query simulation theorem as a base case, we obtain simulation theorems for quantum algorithms with constant rounds of adaptivity.

</details>


### [115] [Group-theoretic treatment of strong light-matter coupling with an arbitrary number of excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20340)
*Antti Peltola,Olli Siltanen,Kimmo Luoma,Konstantinos S. Daskalakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过利用量子发射体的组合结构，研究了具有任意激发数的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导出其本征系统的结构与性质，并识别了现实尺度系统中允许的辐射跃迁，揭示了少激发条件下无法获取的新行为，同时为具有指数增长希尔伯特空间的类似系统提供了降低计算复杂度的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强光-物质相互作用在光学微腔中产生极化激元，虽然已广泛应用于现代技术，但现有理论描述通常局限于单激发情形，无法捕获多激发物理，阻碍了技术进步。

Method: 利用量子发射体的组合结构，系统地研究任意激发数下的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导本征系统的结构与性质，并识别现实尺寸系统中允许的辐射跃迁。

Result: 发现了少激发区域无法接近的新行为，并提供了一个减少具有指数增长希尔伯特空间的类似系统计算复杂度的框架。

Conclusion: 该研究突破了多激发物理的理论限制，揭示了新的物理行为，并为处理指数增长的希尔伯特空间提供了有效的计算框架，有助于推动相关技术的进一步发展。

Abstract: Strong light-matter interactions in optical microcavities give rise to hybrid light-matter states known as polaritons. While actively used in modern technologies, theoretical descriptions of such systems are often restricted to the single-excitation case, limiting their ability to capture many-excitation physics and hindering further technological advancements. Here, by exploiting the combinatorial structure of quantum emitters, we investigate the Tavis-Cummings model with arbitrary number of excitations. We derive the structure and properties of its eigensystem and identify allowed radiative transitions in systems of realistic size scales. Our work reveals new behavior inaccessible to the few-excitation regime, while also providing a framework to reduce the computational complexity of similar systems with exponentially growing Hilbert spaces.

</details>

### [116] [Dynamic Pseudogap Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19592)
*E. Z. Kuchinskii,M. V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个描述赝隙形成的微观动力学理论，其中赝隙由具有特征振荡频率ω₀和阻尼γ的动态有限嵌套矢量Q涨落产生。在Abelian近似下推导了单粒子格林函数的双重级数表示，并发现了一个动态产生的退相干尺度Γ_eff，它控制了相干动态边带和准静态涨落间隙两种赝隙区域之间的交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解赝隙形成的微观机制是凝聚态物理中一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。已有的赝隙理论通常在静态或准静态涨落框架下描述，但实际体系中存在的动态有限嵌套矢量涨落（具有有限频率和阻尼）可能在赝隙形成中起关键作用。作者旨在建立一个统一的微观理论，将动力学涨落与赝隙的形成联系起来，并揭示不同赝隙描述（相干动态边带与准静态涨落间隙）之间的内在联系。

Method: 从电子耦合经典高斯随机场的哈密顿量出发，在SU(2)时间演化的Abelian（交换）近似下，推导了单粒子格林函数的显式双重级数表示。传播子自然地获得广义Bogoliubov结构，每个随机散射历史由有效动态间隙表征，产生具有复Poisson权重的相干叠加动力学展宽边带。通过分析动态产生的退相干尺度Γ_eff与特征频率ω₀的比较，刻画了两种赝隙区域的交叉行为。

Result: 理论的核心结果是动态产生的退相干尺度Γ_eff控制了两个定性的不同的赝隙区域之间的交叉：当ω₀>Γ_eff时，涨落场被相干分辨，双重级数表示提供动态边带形成的受控描述；当Γ_eff≳ω₀时，相干性逐渐丧失，理论交叉到由精确连续分数解描述的准静态涨落间隙区域。数值计算结果在不同模型参数下证实了这一交叉行为，覆盖了广泛的参数范围。

Conclusion: 相干和准静态描述被解释为同一微观动态赝隙模型中两个互补的渐近极限。该理论统一了动态涨落与赝隙形成之间的关系，为理解强关联电子体系中赝隙的微观起源提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We formulate a microscopic theory of pseudogap formation generated by dynamic finite nesting vector ${\bf Q}$ fluctuations with characteristic oscillation frequency $ω_0$, and damping $γ$. Starting from a Hamiltonian describing electrons coupled to a classical Gaussian random field, we derive explicit double-series representations of the single-particle Green's function within an Abelian (commuting) approximation to the exact SU(2) time evolution. The resulting propagator naturally acquires a generalized Bogoliubov structure in which every stochastic scattering history is characterized by an effective dynamic gap, leading to a coherent superposition of dynamically broadened sidebands with complex Poisson weights. A central result of the theory is the emergence of a dynamically generated decoherence scale $Γ_{\rm eff}$ governing the crossover between two qualitatively different pseudogap regimes. For $ω_0>Γ_{\rm eff}$ the fluctuating field is resolved coherently and the double-series representation provides a controlled description of dynamic sideband formation. Conversely, when $Γ_{\rm eff}\gtrsimω_0$, coherence is progressively lost and the theory crosses over to the quasistatic fluctuating-gap regime described by the exact continued-fraction solution. The coherent and quasistatic descriptions are therefore interpreted as two complementary asymptotic limits of the same microscopic dynamic pseudogap model. The detailed results of numerical calculations for electron spectral density and density of states are presented for different sets of model parameters confirming this crossover over the broad range of model parameters.

</details>
